## Review

**7 out of 10, Effective.** Presentation quality, judged with the SlideDeck Co review heuristic (https://slidedeck.co/review-methodology/). The ask, the evidence and the merit of the idea are not rated.

The deck has a consistent brand system and a clear storyline from past to refreshed focus to a 2030 path, and the chart slides (22, 23, 33) put one message in the title and one visual beneath it. Weaknesses are consequential: the 2030 targets slide 15 packs a dense highlights panel and a repeated icon strip into one page, slide 14 labels its equals signs ambiguously, and slides 13 and 15 repeat the same five-step diagram. Several slides use very small source and footnote text (11, 27, 38, 39), and the appendix tables on 43 and 44 are unreadable at presentation size. The forward-looking disclaimer is a wall of tiny text.

**Worth keeping:** Keep the action titles with a green takeaway bar, the single-message chart slides 22 and 23, and the labeled waterfall on slide 33 with CAGR brackets.

**Most useful repair:** Cut the duplicated five-step graphic on slide 15, enlarge or trim the small chart labels and footnotes on slides 27, 39 and 42, and break the appendix reconciliations into readable tables.

**What would change the score:** I would reduce the number of slides that restate the same retention, turnover and growth framework (12, 13, 14, 21) so the argument advances rather than loops.

_Confidence high. 45 of 45 pages inspected on contact sheets, 4 at readable size. Reviewed Reviewed 2026-11-08. Model-assisted review (claude-sonnet-5-5)._

## Summary

The deck opens with a title slide and a forward-looking statements disclaimer, then introduces BrightView as a commercial landscaping and snow services company with about 20,000 employees, $2.8B in revenue and 280 branch locations. It moves through the company's history, showing a prior period of revenue growth without profit growth, the refreshed focus under new leadership, and the 2023 to 2025 adjusted EBITDA trend. Core sections cover the market opportunity, frontline employee turnover, customer retention, development-business growth, share of wallet, national accounts, capital allocation, and a path to about $4B in revenue and 16%+ EBITDA margins by 2030. Operational levers follow, including purchasing, fleet management, and SG&A rebalancing toward sales. The appendix holds FY25 guidance, leadership, and non-GAAP reconciliations. The deck closes with a short thank-you slide. Slides worth studying are the customer retention and turnover correlation charts on slides 22 and 23, which pair a single metric with a clear visual, and the revenue bridge on slide 33 that breaks growth into labeled drivers.

## Questions about this deck

**What does the BrightView investor presentation say about customer retention?**

Slide 22 shows BrightView customer retention moving from about 85% in 2018 to about 81% in 2024, with 2024 marked as the first year of improvement since the IPO. The same slide lists drivers of success including quality of service, communication, and AI-based predictive analytics.

**What are BrightView's 2030 aspirational targets?**

Slide 15 lists 2030 aspirational targets from the February 2025 Investor Day of about $4B in total revenue, about $650M+ in adjusted EBITDA, about 16%+ adjusted EBITDA margins, and about 40%+ free cash flow conversion.

**What is BrightView's FY25 revenue and EBITDA guidance?**

Slide 17 gives FY25 guidance from the November 2024 earnings call of total revenue of $2.750B to $2.840B and adjusted EBITDA of $335M to $355M. The same slide lists adjusted free cash flow guidance of $40M to $60M.

**How big is the U.S. market that BrightView operates in, and what share does it hold?**

Slide 10 places the U.S. commercial landscaping services market at about $88B, with BrightView at about $1.7B (land only) and a market share of about 2%. The slide cites IBIS Industry Reports (April 2024) as the source.

**Layout**

**Visuals**

BrightView logo: 'BrightView' in blue rounded bold sans with a green stylised sprout/tree glyph and registered mark at upper right. Short green horizontal bar above the title. Stacked stock badge 'BV / LISTED / NYSE' with thin blue horizontal rules between the lines. Photograph on the right: two landscaping workers in hi-vis yellow-green safety vests with grey reflective stripes crouching and planting; the foreground worker wears a turquoise BrightView cap, dark sunglasses, a blue neck gaiter and a grey work glove holding a trowel; the second worker at right wears a grey cap and sunglasses. Green shrubs in the foreground, blurred green-glass building behind, bright daylight, dominant yellow-green, blue and white. Photo is cropped to a diagonal parallelogram edge, with two semi-transparent white diagonal bands (35% and 60% opacity) overlapping the left edge of the photo.

a determination by customers to reduce their outsourcing or use of preferred vendors; inconsistent practices and the operating results of individual branches; our ability to implement our business strategies and achieve our growth objectives; impacts of future acquisitions or other strategic transactions; the possibility that costs or difficulties related to the integration of acquired businesses’ operations will be greater than expected and the possibility that integration efforts will disrupt our business and strain management time and resources; the potential impacts on revenues and our financial condition caused by any disposition of assets or discontinuation of lines of business; the seasonal nature of our landscape maintenance services; our dependence on weather conditions and the impact of severe weather and climate change on our business; disruptions in our supply chain and changes in our ability to source adequate supplies and materials in a timely manner; any failure to accurately estimate the overall risk, requirements, or costs when we bid on or negotiate contracts that are ultimately awarded to us; the conditions and periodic fluctuations of the new commercial construction sector, as well as spending on repair and upgrade activities; the level, timing and location of snowfall; our ability to retain or hire our executive management and other key personnel; our ability to attract, retain and maintain positive relations with workers; any failure to properly verify employment eligibility of our employees; the liability exposure from our use of subcontractors to perform work under certain customer contracts; the distraction and impact caused by litigation, of adverse litigation judgments and settlements resulting from legal proceedings; tax increases and changes in tax rules; any increase in on-job accidents involving employees; any failure, inadequacy, interruption, security failure or breach of our information technology systems; compliance with data privacy regulations; our ability to adequately protect our intellectual property; any adverse consequences of our substantial indebtedness; increases in interest rates governing our variable rate indebtedness increasing the cost of servicing our substantial indebtedness; risks related to counterparty credit worthiness or non-performance of the derivative financial instruments we utilize; restrictions within our debt agreements that limit our flexibility in operating; our ability to generate sufficient cash flow to satisfy our significant debt service obligations; the incurrence of substantially more debt, including off-balance sheet financing, contractual obligations and general and commercial liabilities; any failure to extend credit under our facility or reduce the borrowing base under our Revolving Credit Facility; any future sales, or the perception of future sales, by us or our affiliates, which could cause the market price for our common stock to decline; the ability of KKR and One Rock to exert significant influence over us; anti-takeover provisions in our organizational documents that could delay or prevent a change in control; the authorization of our Board of Directors to issue and designate shares of our preferred stock in additional series without stockholder approval; the fact that the holders of our Series A Preferred Stock may have different interests from and vote their shares in a manner deemed adverse to, holders of our common stock; the dividend, liquidation, and redemption rights of the holders of our Series A Preferred Stock; ### Slide 3

**Text**

BrightView Today
Founded in 1939
Headquartered: Blue Bell, PA
Employees: ~20,000
Revenue: $2.8B
Jobs: ~20,000
Branch Locations: 280
Note: Branch locations include satellites
States with BrightView Branches
Corporate Headquarters
Maintenance Branches
Development Branches
Shared Branches
Business Overview & Highlights
Essential Commercial Landscaping & Snow Services
Predictable recurring revenue model
Non-discretionary and resilient service
Broad offering of ancillary services
Landscape Architecture & Development Services
New landscapes / large-scale redesign projects
Horticultural & design thought-leadership
Complex and high-profile projects
3

**Layout**

Title top-left at about 10% from top. Upper area has a light grey box with a six-bullet list on the left, a US map in the centre-right and a legend to the right of the map. A full-width blue banner heading spans the middle. Below it are two bullet columns separated by a dotted vertical line. BrightView logo sits bottom-left and the page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

A map of the United States (with Alaska and Hawaii insets) shows states with BrightView branches in muted purple (#7B7BA8) and states without branches in light grey (#B3B6B8). Small dots mark branches: dark navy for maintenance, orange for development and light green for shared. Dense clusters appear in California, Arizona, Texas, Florida and the Northeast corridor. A yellow star marks the headquarters near Pennsylvania. The legend to the right has a purple square, a yellow star, a navy dot, an orange dot and a green dot, each beside its label. A blue gradient banner (#0072CE at top fading to dark navy #004A8F at bottom) holds white bold text. A vertical dotted grey line separates the two lower columns. The BrightView logo has blue wordmark text with a green stylised tree/leaf mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold Arial blue #0072CE at about 5.2% of slide height. Body text in Arial grey #63666A at about 3% of slide height. Bullets are green #8BC400. The left column heading is bold green #8BC400, and the right column heading is bold blue #0072CE. The info box is light grey #F2F2F2 with square corners and no shadow. The banner has a vertical gradient from bright blue to dark navy. The footer note is small grey text. The page number is bold green.

**Layout**

Left-aligned title near top. Below it two full-width horizontal bands: a blue Maintenance band with a 6x2 grid of icons with centered labels, and a teal Development band with a single row of 8 icons with centered labels. Each band has a vertical rotated label in a darker gradient strip at its left edge. BrightView logo at bottom left, green page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

White line-art icons above each label. Maintenance row 1: person on a riding mower; kneeling gardener planting a sprout; wheelbarrow of mulch; snow blower with spray; worker with chainsaw in a tree; sprinkler/irrigation head with water droplets. Row 2: lake with trees and reeds; potted plant with shovel and rake; house hit by a tornado with flood waves; sports stadium with field; golf flag on a green with trees; blueprint with triangle ruler and pencil. Development row: blueprint with set square and pencil; potted plant with shovel and rake; brick wall with trowel; cement mixer truck; sprinkler head; pool with water waves in a tiled frame; hand holding soil with sprouting plant; sports stadium. Left vertical strips are blue-to-dark-blue and teal-to-dark-teal gradient images. BrightView logo: blue wordmark with a green stylised leaf/tree mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title bold blue #0072CE Arial bold sans-serif, about 5.2% of slide height. Maintenance band blue #0072CE, Development band teal #00AD97, both with square corners and no shadows. Labels and icons white, Arial bold, about 2.6% of slide height. Vertical band labels white Arial bold about 3.7%. Page number green #8BC400.

### Slide 5

**Text**

BrightView’s Early Days…
Combined Two / Legacy / Companies / to Create a / National Presence
Initial Public / Offering
Not Growing / Profitability, / Limited Cash, / Stuck In Place
2014
2018
2019–2022
2023
2024
2025+
Focused on Revenue Growth, but not Profitable Growth
BrightView
5

**Layout**

Title at top left. A rising diagonal chevron timeline runs from lower left to upper right across the middle, with captions and circular icons above and below the chevrons. A full-width green banner sits near the bottom, with the BrightView logo at bottom left and the slide number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Six chevron arrows in a row, rotated about 14 degrees upward to the right. Each carries a white bold year label. Fills: 2014 dark teal #005559, 2018 teal #007177, 2019–2022 bright teal #00AD97, 2023 dark blue #00569B, 2024 blue #0072CE, 2025+ green gradient #8BC400 to #6AB43E. Three white chevron separators sit between the arrows. Three circular line icons with thin teal outlines: a handshake with radiating lines under 2014 (dark teal), a dollar sign with an upward trend arrow over stacked coins above 2018 (teal), and a dollar coin with three downward arrows under 2019–2022 (bright teal). A green gradient banner holds white bold text. The BrightView logo has blue wordmark text and a green stylised B tree mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bright blue #0072CE, bold Arial-style sans-serif, about 5% of slide height. Captions in regular Arial sans-serif, about 3% of slide height, coloured teal #005559, #007177 and #00AD97 to match the chevrons. Banner text is white bold Arial at about 3.7% of slide height. The banner is a flat rectangle with a light-to-darker green vertical gradient and no rounded corners. The page number is green #8BC400 bold. No shadows.

6

**Layout**

Title at top left. Two equal bordered chart panels side by side beneath the title, each with a bold italic grey heading at top left. A footnote sits under the left panel, the BrightView logo is at bottom left, and the slide number is at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left panel: bar chart of Revenue ($Ms) with four solid blue bars for 2018, 2019, 2021 and 2022. Printed values are 2,354, 2,405, 2,554 and 2,775. There is a grey baseline and no y-axis. An elliptical callout reading '+~$420M or +18%' sits on a grey bracket line that goes from above the 2018 bar across to an arrowhead pointing down at the 2,775 label. Right panel: combo chart of Adj. EBITDA ($Ms) and EBITDA % with four mustard-gold bars for 2018, 2019, 2021 and 2022. Printed bar values are 300, 305, 302 and 288. A green line runs across the bars with percentage labels 12.8%, 12.7%, 11.8% and 10.4%; the 11.8% and 10.4% labels have white backgrounds. The line starts near the top of 2018, stays roughly flat to 2019, then slopes down steeply to 2021 and 2022. The right axis (15.0 to 10.0) and left axis (380 to 250) are hidden by white fills, so no axis is visible. An elliptical callout reading '(~$12M) & (240bps)' sits on a grey bracket line going from the 2018 point to an arrowhead pointing down at the 288 bar. The legend below the chart has a green line swatch labelled 'Adj. EBITDA %' and a gold square labelled 'Adjusted EBITDA'. Each panel has a thin light grey border. The BrightView logo is blue wordmark text with a green stylised tree/leaf mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. The title is bold blue #0072CE Arial. Chart text is regular Arial in grey #63666A, with headings in bold italic. Revenue bars are blue #0072CE, EBITDA bars are gold #D59320, and the line is green #1E9B0B. Panel borders are thin light grey #BFBFBF. The callout ellipses have white fill and a thin grey outline. The footnote is small italic dark grey #3B3B3B. The slide number is bold green #8BC400. No shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

White slide with a bold blue title at top left. A rising diagonal arrow timeline of six chevron segments runs from lower left to upper right across the middle. Caption text and circular icons alternate above and below the segments. A full-width green banner sits near the bottom, with the BrightView logo at bottom left and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Six chevron arrow segments tilted upward, left to right, in a gradient of colours: 2014 dark teal #005559, 2018 teal #007177, 2019–2022 bright teal-green #00AD97, 2023 dark blue #00569B, 2024 bright blue #0072CE, 2025+ green gradient (#8BC400 to #6AB82F) with an arrow tip. Each has a white bold year label rotated along the incline. Circular outline icons: (1) dark teal circle with a handshake and radiating lines, below the 2014 segment; (2) teal circle with a dollar sign, upward trend arrow and coin stacks, above 2018; (3) green-teal circle with a dollar coin and three downward arrows, below 2019–2022; (4) dark blue circle with a person figure above three stars, above 2023; (5) bright blue circle with 'ONE BV' text in blue and a small puzzle-like icon, below 2024; (6) lime green circle with a hand holding a rising bar chart with an arrow, above 2025+. Full-width green gradient banner (#8BC400 to #7DB92E) with white bold text. BrightView wordmark in blue with a green stylised leaf/tree mark at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold Arial blue #0072CE, about 5.2% of slide height. Caption text in regular Arial about 3% of slide height, coloured to match each segment (dark teal #005559, teal #007177, #00AD97, navy blue #00569B, blue #0072CE, lime #8BC400). Banner text in white bold Arial about 3.7%. Flat shapes with thin white separator outlines on chevrons, no shadows. Icons drawn as thin line art inside circles with 2–3px outline strokes matching the segment colour.

8

**Layout**

White slide with a left-aligned blue title near the top, a full-width green banner beneath it, then two equal bordered chart panels side by side (left revenue, right Adj. EBITDA). Footnotes sit below the panels at left, the BrightView logo at bottom left and the slide number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left panel: bar chart 'Revenue ($Ms)' with three blue bars for 2023 (2,816), 2024 (2,767) and 2025* (2,795), a grey baseline, no gridlines or y-axis. A grey bracket line runs from above the 2023 bar to an arrowhead above the 2025* bar, with an oval labelled '~Flat' on it. Right panel: bar chart 'Adj. EBITDA ($Ms) & EBITDA %' with three gold bars for 2023 (299), 2024 (325) and 2025* (345), with small tick marks under the value labels. A green line with white-backed labels connects 10.6% (2023), 11.7% (2024) and 12.3% (2025*) across the bars. A grey bracket with an oval labelled '+~$45M & +170bps' runs from above the 2023 bar to an arrowhead above the 2025* bar. The hidden secondary axis is white-covered (values 10.0–15.0 and 240–380). Legend at the bottom: green line 'Adj. EBITDA %' and gold square 'Adjusted EBITDA'. BrightView logo at bottom left: blue wordmark with a green stylised leaf/B mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean corporate style with a white background. Title is bright blue #0072CE in bold Arial at about 5.2% of slide height. The banner is a vertical gradient from light green #8CC63F at the top to #4DB045 at the bottom, with white bold Arial text at about 3.7% of height. Chart text is grey #63666A in Arial at about 2.6%. Panel headers are bold italic Arial with italic units. Panels have thin light grey #BFBFBF borders with square corners and no shadow. Footnotes are italic Arial in dark grey #3B3B3B at about 1.5%. The page number is bold green #8BC400.

### Slide 9

**Text**

Key Investment Highlights
Unifying BrightView
Market leader with size, scale and strategy to / compound competitive advantages
Prioritizing our employees to become employer and / service provider of choice
Sustainable business model with recurring revenue / and intense customer focus to gain wallet share
Multiple strategic initiatives including leveraging our / size and scale to drive profitable growth
Clear path to achieve long-term targets and drive / shareholder value
BrightView of the Past Will Not Be the BrightView of the Future
BrightView
9

**Layout**

Title at top left. Full-width blue banner below the title, then a left column of five icon-plus-text rows and a right-hand photo box. Full-width green banner near the bottom, BrightView logo at bottom left, page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Blue gradient banner (darker at the bottom) across the width with centred white bold text. Five circular blue-outlined icons stacked vertically at the left: 1) award rosette with check mark; 2) three people figures above an upward zigzag arrow; 3) circular arrows forming a cycle; 4) three overlapping circles (Venn); 5) bar chart with rising dotted line and arrow. Right: a photograph in a thin grey border of an asphalt road curving into the distance through green trees and grass under a blue sky with large white clouds; white edge lines and painted white numbers 2026, 2025 and 2024 on the road, growing larger toward the viewer; the road is at the centre, with sunlight and saturated greens and blues. Green gradient banner (light green at the top to darker green at the bottom) with white bold text. BrightView logo: blue wordmark with a green stylised B mark at the right. Small green page number.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold blue #0072CE Arial, about 5.2% of slide height. Banner text white bold Arial about 3.7%. Body text in grey #63666A regular Arial, about 3% of slide height, with tight line spacing. Icons are line-art in blue #0072CE with circular outlines about 2px thick. Blue banner gradient from #0072CE to #00528F; green banner gradient from #8CC63F to #5FAE3A. No shadows or rounded corners on the banners. Page number green #8BC400.

**Layout**

Left-aligned title at top with a short green rule beneath, then a grey subtitle spanning the width. Below is a 4-column by 2-row grid of circular icons with labels, a dotted divider, and a line chart section in the lower half. The footer has the BrightView logo at bottom left, a footnote beside it and the page number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Eight circular outline icons in a 4x2 grid. Green (#8BC400) circles with line-art icons: a pickup truck, a work boot, a person pushing a lawn mower, a clock with a person (service hours), a hand holding a dollar coin (affordable benefits), and a calendar with a schedule. Green filled circular checkmarks sit beside Trucks, Boots, Mowers, Service Hours, Affordable Benefits and Flexible Schedules. Two blue (#0072CE) circles show a calendar with a check (PTO) and a gear with a person (HRIS*), with no checkmarks. A dotted light-grey horizontal divider runs across the slide at about 49% height. Below it is a line chart with no y-axis: a blue line falls from about 67% height at Sep ’23 through about 69% at Dec ’23 and Mar ’24, about 71% at Jun ’24 and about 76% at Sep ’24 to about 79% at Dec ’24, above a thin grey x-axis line with six labelled months. A dark grey bracket line starts at a vertical tick above the Sep ’23 point and runs horizontally through a teal-blue gradient outlined ellipse reading 'Turnover down ~20% over 5 quarters', continuing to the green circle at right. A green gradient circle at right (#8BC400 to #4CAF50) holds white bold text, with a dark grey arrow pointing down from it to the Dec ’24 end of the line. The BrightView logo is blue text with a green stylised leaf mark at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bright blue #0072CE bold Arial, about 5.2% of slide height. Subtitle in grey #63666A bold Arial, about 3.7%. Icon labels in lime green #8BC400 bold Arial 3.3%, with PTO and HRIS* in regular grey. Chart heading in blue bold. Ellipse text in dark blue #00569B bold. Circle text is white bold at 2.6%. Axis labels are regular grey Arial about 3%. Green accent bar. The ellipse has a thick gradient outline from navy #00569B to teal #00A19B and a white fill. No shadows.

### Slide 12

**Text**

Driving Profitable Growth…
Profitable Growth
New Sales / Growth
Development / Growth
Development / Conversions
Ancillary / Offerings
National / Accounts
CUSTOMER RETENTION
BrightView
12

**Layout**

Title top-left, left-aligned. Below it a temple graphic spans nearly full width: a grey triangular pediment on top, five equal columns in a row beneath it, and a wide gradient base bar labelled CUSTOMER RETENTION. BrightView logo sits bottom-left and the page number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Temple/pillar diagram. A dark grey triangular pediment with a vertical gradient (lighter grey #707275 at top to darker #48494C at bottom) holds the label 'Profitable Growth'. Five trapezoid pillars with capitals and bases, each with a vertical gradient and a white circular icon badge near the top. Pillar 1 is blue (#0072CE to #004282) with an icon of a dollar-sign coin with rays above three upward arrows. Pillar 2 is dark teal (#009AA0 to #004F55) with an icon of a checklist clipboard and two gears. Pillar 3 is turquoise-green (#00C9B0 to #009A8A) with an icon of two curved arrows, one pointing left and one right, forming an exchange loop. Pillar 4 is lime green (#8DC600 to #5F8A00) with an icon of a gear containing an exclamation mark above an open hand. Pillar 5 is pale green (#C4E28A to #A5D55A) with an icon of a US map with a hashtag and a group of three people above it. Capitals and bases are flat colour trapezoids in each pillar's colour, with darker thin strips. The base bar spans the pillars with a horizontal gradient from navy blue (#004282) through teal (#00A39A) and lime (#8DC600) to pale green (#C4E28A). The BrightView logo has blue wordmark text and a green stylised tree-like mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bright blue #0072CE, bold Arial-style sans-serif, about 5% of slide height. Labels inside the pillars, pediment and base are bold white Arial, about 3-4.4% of slide height. The base label is in all caps. The page number is small bold green #8BC400. Gradients on the pillars, pediment and base; no shadows; sharp corners.

### Slide 13

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: the 2030 targets slide 15 packs a dense highlights panel and a repeated icon strip into one page, slide 14 labels its equals signs ambiguously, and slides 13 and 15 repeat the same five-step diagram.

**Text**

…by Operating as One BrightView
Early in the / Journey of / Transforming / BrightView
BECOMING / THE / EMPLOYER / OF CHOICE
CUSTOMER / CENTRIC / FOCUS
UNLOCKING / SIZE AND / SCALE
STRATEGIC / CAPITAL / ALLOCATION
INVESTMENT / OF CHOICE
13

**Layout**

Title at top left. Below, a horizontal band: a dark blue-to-teal chevron panel on the left, then five equal vertical cards with circular icons at top, separated by grey plus signs and a final equals sign, over a light grey strip. BrightView logo bottom left, page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left panel: pentagon/arrow-shaped block with a gradient from navy blue at top to teal at bottom, pointed edge on the right. Light grey (#F2F2F2) horizontal strip behind the circles. Five tall pale-tinted rounded-top cards, each with a coloured bar at the bottom and a 113px circular gradient badge with a white ring and white line icon. Card 1: teal circle, person with star on chest and sun rays. Card 2: blue circle, two people shaking hands with a star above. Card 3: dark teal circle, coin with dollar sign above a seesaw/scale with a hand. Card 4: light blue circle, donut/pie chart with a dollar sign at the centre. Card 5: green circle, hand holding a plant sprout with two dollar coins. Grey plus signs between circles 1-2, 2-3, 3-4 and a grey equals sign between 4 and 5. BrightView logo: blue wordmark with a green tree icon.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bright blue #0072CE bold Arial sans-serif, about 5.2% of slide height. Panel text white bold Arial about 3.3%. Card labels bold uppercase Arial about 3% in teal #009684, blue #0072CE, dark teal #007177, light blue #61BFE5, green #00B050. Cards are pale tints (#CEF0EB, #CEE4F6, #CEE4E5, #E5F4FC, #CEF0DD) with a 0.8% high solid bottom bar. Circle badges use gradients. Page number green #8BC400.

**Layout**

Title at top left. A tall blue-to-teal pentagon (arrow-shaped) panel sits at the left, spanning the lower two thirds of the slide. To its right are three rows, separated by two dotted horizontal lines. Each row has a circled arrow icon, a label, an equals badge in the centre, then a circled arrow icon and label on the right. Logo bottom left, page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left: a vertical pentagon/chevron-pointing-right shape with a gradient from dark blue at top to teal-green at bottom. Six white circles with blue outlines (about 3px, #0072CE), each holding a green gradient block arrow: row 1 left arrow points down, all other five point up. Three small circular badges with a dark blue to teal gradient, each with a white equals sign. Two light grey dotted horizontal lines between rows. BrightView logo at bottom left: blue wordmark with a green stylised tree/leaf mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bright blue #0072CE, Arial bold, about 5.2% of slide height. Panel text white Arial bold about 4.8%. Row labels in grey #63666A Arial bold about 4.8%. Green arrows from #8BC400 to #4CAF50 gradients. Panel gradient from #004680 to #00A592. Page number green #8BC400, small bold.

### Slide 15

**Review notes**

- Review: Weaknesses are consequential: the 2030 targets slide 15 packs a dense highlights panel and a repeated icon strip into one page, slide 14 labels its equals signs ambiguously, and slides 13 and 15 repeat the same five-step diagram.
- Suggested repair: Cut the duplicated five-step graphic on slide 15, enlarge or trim the small chart labels and footnotes on slides 27, 39 and 42, and break the appendix reconciliations into readable tables.

**Text**

2030 Aspirational Targets (from February ’25 Investor Day)
Clear path to / achieve long-term / targets and drive / shareholder value
~$4B*+
Total Revenue
~$650M+
Adjusted EBITDA
~16%+

~40%+
Free Cash Flow / Conversion**

BECOMING / THE / EMPLOYER / OF CHOICE
CUSTOMER / CENTRIC / FOCUS
UNLOCKING / SIZE AND / SCALE

INVESTMENT / OF CHOICE

15

**Layout**

White slide with title at top left and a thin green rule beneath it, a target icon at the top right. Middle band: left column with a green accent bar, headline and a photo; a 2x2 grid of green metric tiles; a blue panel at the right with a 2x2 grid of highlights. Bottom band: five circular icons with labels joined by plus and equals signs. Footer holds the BrightView logo at bottom left, footnotes beside it and the slide number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Green target icon with a dart at top right, drawn as concentric lime-green spiral rings with an arrow pointing in from the left. Rendering of a modern pale stone museum tower with trees, a plaza and people walking, soft overcast light, boxed at left. Four green gradient tiles with white text. Blue highlights panel with short white dividers above each of the four highlights. Light grey band behind the bottom icon row. Five circular icons, each with a tinted label card and a coloured bar at the bottom: teal circle with a person and star, navy circle with two people shaking hands and a star, dark teal circle with a scale and a hand holding a dollar coin, light blue circle with a segmented dollar-sign wheel, green circle with a hand holding a plant with dollar coins. Grey plus signs sit between the circles and a grey equals sign follows the fourth. BrightView logo with a green sprout mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold blue #0072CE, with the subtitle in smaller bold blue. Headline text in bold grey #63666A. Green gradient tiles from #8BC400 to #5CB335 with white bold figures and regular white labels. Blue panel #0072CE with white text. Bottom labels are bold uppercase in each icon's colour (teal #009684, blue #0072CE, dark teal #007177, light blue #61BFE5, green #00B050). Typeface is Arial throughout. Thin lime rule #8BC400 under the title. No rounded corners. Footnotes are small grey #63666A and the page number is bold green.

White slide with title top left, a blue subtitle below, a row of five evenly spaced circular headshots with centered names and titles beneath, a dotted divider, then a section heading, a green circle at left and a grid of four columns by two rows of names and regions. BrightView logo bottom left, page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Five circular headshot photos with teal-to-blue ring borders against light grey studio backdrops: a smiling grey-haired man with glasses in a navy blazer; a dark-haired man in a navy suit and light blue shirt; a blonde woman with long wavy hair in black; a man with swept-back hair in a navy blazer and patterned blue shirt; a grey-haired man in a dark jacket and striped shirt. A horizontal grey dotted line separates the sections. A green gradient circle, light green at top to darker green at bottom, holds white bold text. BrightView logo with blue wordmark and a green stylized B mark at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. Titles and headings in bright blue #0072CE, Arial bold. Executive names in blue #0070C0 bold capitals. Job titles and regions in grey #63666A regular Arial. Sales leader names in teal #007177 bold. Green circle gradient #8BC400 to #3AAA35 with white bold text. Page number green #8BC400. Photo rings are a gradient from dark blue #00508F to teal #008A9B. Title about 5.2% of slide height, subheadings 3.7%, names 2.6%, roles 2.2%.

### Slide 19

**Text**

Our People are Our Differentiator
Building a Strong, Dedicated & Unified One BrightView
~20,000 Total / Employees
Reduced Seasonal / Hiring Needs Due / to Improvement in / Frontline Turnover
Tenured Frontline / Crew Leader: ~8 Yrs. / Crew: ~4 Yrs.
Competitive Wages / with Reliable Work / Schedules
BrightView is the Only National Landscaping Company Utilizing the
E-Verify Standard Enterprise-wide
BrightView
19

**Layout**

White slide with the title top-left and a short green rule beneath it, then a grey subtitle. A three-column band follows: two stacked teal boxes on the left, a wide photo in the centre, two stacked teal boxes on the right. A full-width green banner sits below, with the BrightView logo bottom-left and the page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Centre photo of about 20 BrightView crew members in hi-vis yellow-green vests and blue shirts, standing in a wide arc and stretching sideways in a gravel lot. White BrightView pickup trucks and trailers are parked behind them, with tall green trees under a hazy sky. The light is warm. A thin grey border frames the photo. A white banner overlaid at top centre shows a faint American flag background and the blue and red E-Verify logo with a small flag icon. Four teal boxes with a vertical gradient from bright teal to darker green-teal. A green banner with a gradient from lime to darker green. A short lime green rule under the title. The BrightView logo is blue wordmark with a green stylised leaf/B glyph.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold blue Arial (#0072CE) at about 5.2% of slide height. Subtitle in bold grey (#63666A) at about 3.7%. Box text is bold white Arial at about 3.3%, centred. Banner text is white Arial, with a regular first line and a bold second line. Page number is bold green (#8BC400). Boxes and banner have square corners, no shadows, and gradient fills.

### Slide 20

**Text**

Putting the Customer at the Center of what we do
Scale and / Technology / Advantages
Go-to-Market / Strategy
CUSTOMER CENTRIC FOCUS
Capturing / Share of Wallet
Revenue / Growth Drivers
20

**Layout**

Title top-left. A circular cycle diagram sits in the center with four icon-and-label groups at the corners: top-left, top-right, bottom-left and bottom-right. Dotted lines link each label to the ring. The BrightView logo is bottom-left and the page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Center ring made of four curved light-grey gradient arrow segments (#F2F2F2 to #D6D6D6) with white gaps between them. Blue triangle arrowheads (#0072CE) point right at top, down at right, left at bottom and up at left, so the flow runs clockwise. Inside the ring is a blue gradient circle (#0072BC to #00448A) with a thin white inner outline ring. It holds a white line icon of two people shaking hands with a star above them. Grey curved text 'CUSTOMER CENTRIC FOCUS' runs along the inner lower arc of the ring. Four blue line icons: top-left, devices (monitor, tablet and phone with expand arrows); top-right, a rocket launching; bottom-left, two hands around a target; bottom-right, a person with a magnifier beside a rising bar chart with a dollar coin. Each icon has a solid blue bar beneath it. Dotted grey horizontal lines end in grey dots on the ring, at about y 33% (top pair) and y 73% (bottom pair). The BrightView logo is blue text with a green stylised tree/B mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold Arial-style sans-serif, blue #0072CE, about 5% of slide height. Labels in regular Arial, grey #63666A, about 3.3% of slide height. Accent blue #0072CE for icons, bars and arrowheads. Page number in green #8BC400, bold. Ring has soft grey gradients with no outlines. Dotted connector lines are grey #999999.

21

**Layout**

Title at top left. Below it, six evenly spaced rows separated by dotted grey lines. Each row has a numbered leaf icon at left, a blue bold two-line (or one-line) heading, a thin vertical grey divider, a grey triangle arrow and grey description text. A full-width green banner sits below the rows. The BrightView logo is at bottom left and the slide number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Six leaf icons, each a teal-to-navy gradient leaf shape with a white number (1-6) on it and a curved vein line. Six light grey right-pointing triangles beside the descriptions. Six thin vertical grey divider lines between headings and descriptions. Five horizontal dotted grey separator lines between rows. A green gradient banner from light lime at top to darker green at bottom. BrightView logo in blue text with a green stylised leaf/tree mark at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bright blue #0072CE, bold Arial-style sans-serif, about 5.2% of slide height. Row headings bold blue #0072CE at about 3.3%. Descriptions regular grey #63666A at about 3%. Banner text white bold, with the figure larger at about 5.9%. Leaf icons gradient from navy #0B4F8A to teal #1BA89A. Banner gradient #9BCB3C to #4DAE40. Slide number green #8BC400. No shadows, square corners on the banner.

### Slide 22

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: A long bar series from 2018 to 2024 is annotated with a best-in-class benchmark and a first-improvement callout, which makes the trend readable at a glance.
- Worth keeping: Keep the action titles with a green takeaway bar, the single-message chart slides 22 and 23, and the labeled waterfall on slide 33 with CAGR brackets.

**Text**

Opportunities Abound within Customer Retention
~85%
~84%
~83%
~83%
~80%
~79%
~81%
90%+
ONE BV
First Year of / Improvement / Since IPO
2018
2019
2020
2021
2022
2023
2024
BV Best in-Class
Drivers of Success
Quality of Service
Prioritizing our employees so / they are enabled to provide / superior customer service
Communication
Proactively responding to customers’ / shifting priorities with reliable service / delivery enabled by communication and / BrightView Connect
Leverage AI for / Predictive Analytics
Investing in technology to / target at-risk customers / before they terminate

22

**Layout**

Title top-left aligned on a white slide. Below it, a line chart spans the width with year labels along a grey axis at about 62% height. A green 'Drivers of Success' banner sits above a light grey panel split into three columns by dotted dividers. BrightView logo is bottom-left and the page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Line chart of customer retention by year, 2018 to 2024 plus a 'BV Best in-Class' target. A thin blue line connects circular blue markers with white bold labels: 2018 ~85%, 2019 ~84%, 2020 ~83%, 2021 ~83%, 2022 ~80%, 2023 ~79%. The 2024 marker is teal and labelled ~81%. From it a blue line rises steeply to a green scalloped starburst badge labelled 90%+ at the BV Best in-Class position. A large blue circle with the 'ONE BV' logo (ONE in lime green with a small multicolour puzzle icon, BV in large white bold letters) sits above 2023-2024, connected by a vertical grey dotted line down to the line. Teal text 'First Year of Improvement Since IPO' sits under the 2024 marker. A grey horizontal axis line sits above the year labels. Below, a green gradient banner (lighter lime at top to darker green at bottom) reads 'Drivers of Success'. Three blue line icons: a medal/award ribbon with a star next to Quality of Service; two speech bubbles with arrows next to Communication; a head-profile brain with circuit nodes next to Leverage AI. Two vertical grey dotted dividers separate the columns. The BrightView logo is blue bold text with a green stylised B-shaped mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background with a blue and green corporate palette. The title is bold blue #0072CE Arial, about 5.2% of slide height. Chart labels are white bold on blue circles. Axis labels are grey #63666A regular Arial at about 2.6%. Column headings are bold blue at about 2.8%. Body text is grey #63666A at about 2.4%. The banner is a green gradient #8BC53F to #5DB037 with white bold text. The bottom panel is light grey #F2F2F2. No shadows and no rounded corners.

- Worth studying: Customer retention is plotted against frontline turnover quartiles with a single bracketed gap label, so the relationship is shown in one comparison.
- Worth keeping: Keep the action titles with a green takeaway bar, the single-message chart slides 22 and 23, and the labeled waterfall on slide 33 with CAGR brackets.

Customer Retention

23

**Layout**

Left-aligned two-line bold blue title at top left with a short green rule beneath it, then a blue subtitle. A combination chart fills the middle and lower area, with a circular callout at top centre of the chart. A legend sits below the chart, with the BrightView logo bottom left, the footnote beside it and the page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Combination chart titled 'Customer Retention % by Turnover Quartile'. The x-axis has four categories: 4th Quartile, 3rd Quartile, 2nd Quartile, 1st Quartile, with a thin grey baseline. The blue columns show Frontline Turnover* and have no printed values, with heights decreasing from left to right: about 4th Quartile 55% of plot height, 3rd about 37%, 2nd about 23%, 1st about 13%. An orange line with round markers shows Customer Retention on a secondary axis (about 70 to 85, no axis labels visible); labelled points are 78% (4th Quartile, white bold label inside the blue column) and 84% (1st Quartile, grey bold label under the marker); the middle points are about 81 (3rd Quartile) and about 82 (2nd Quartile). A dark grey bracket line runs from the 78% marker up and across to the circular callout, then from the callout across and down with an arrowhead to the 84% marker. The callout is a white circle with a thick gradient ring going from dark navy at the top to teal-green at the bottom. The circle contains '~600bps Difference' in dark blue. The legend has an orange line with a marker labelled Customer Retention and a blue square labelled Frontline Turnover*. A short lime-green horizontal bar sits under the title. The BrightView logo has blue wordmark text and a green stylised tree icon.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title and subtitle in bright blue #0072CE, bold Arial/Helvetica-style sans-serif; title about 5.2% of slide height and subtitle about 3.3%. Accents are lime green #8BC400 (rule and page number), orange #F7941D (line), and blue #0072CE (columns). The callout text is dark blue #00569B bold. Chart labels and legend are grey #63666A Arial regular at about 2.6% of slide height. The footnote is small grey text. No shadows. The callout ring has a gradient from #00457C to #00A99D.

**Visuals**

Left: donut chart 'Branch Segmentation*' with four segments clockwise from 12 o'clock: dark blue (#0072CE) about 20% labelled ~20%, light blue (#85C9FF) 20% labelled ~20%, pale green (#B8D97D) 40% labelled ~40%, bright green (#8BC400) 20% labelled ~20%. A short bright green horizontal rule sits under the title. The BrightView logo at bottom left has blue wordmark text and a green stylised leaf/tree mark.

**Layout**

Title at top left, with a short green rule below it. A two-line subtitle sits below the rule, left-aligned. The lower half has a boxed photo on the left, a circular map zoom in the centre-right and a four-item legend on the right. The BrightView logo sits bottom left and the page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left: a rectangular photograph of a person seen from behind, in dark clothing, looking at a desktop monitor. The background is blurred. The monitor shows a Power BI style dashboard, with a blue top toolbar (Export, Chat in Teams, Get insights, Set alert, Copilot), filter row, KPI tiles (20.3K, $1.7bn, 3K, $1.7bn, 20.3K, $1.7bn, 9.6%), a US map covered in dots in green, magenta, blue and dark green, and a data table on the right. Colourful sticky notes lie at the bottom right of the desk. A small green circle on the US map's west coast links to a long green horizontal arrow pointing right to a circular zoom. The circle is a satellite map of San Pablo Bay with a green outline. It has coloured dots in magenta, blue and lime green, a large green ellipse, a red line with a blue label '~25 miles', and the water label 'San Pablo Bay'. Right: four legend circles with labels, in dark green (Branch), lime green (Existing Customer), blue (Vetted Target) and magenta (Unvetted Target). The BrightView logo is bottom left, in blue text with a green stylised leaf mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. The title is bold Arial-style sans-serif, blue #0072CE, about 5% of slide height. The subtitle is bold grey #63666A, about 3.7%. The '= Profitable Growth' line is bold lime green #8BC400, about 4.5%. The legend text is regular Arial in grey #63666A, about 2.6%. The page number is bold green. A short lime green bar sits under the title. The circular map has a lime green outline of about 3px. There are no shadows or gradients.

**Layout**

**Visuals**

Left: six-row table with a blue heading rule, light green-tinted first column (#1, 30, 1,000+, ~$1M, ~90%, 2,500+) and grey horizontal row dividers. Under the table are three photos side by side: an aerial view of a circular modern campus building with green lawn and trees; a landscaped garden with a stone lantern, stepped stone wall, lily pond, grasses and trees; a dusk playground scene with a curved wooden lattice canopy structure, lit in purple and red, and children playing. Middle bar chart 'Development Backlog ($MS)': blue bar $880 at 10/1/2024, a very thin green line at the middle position, green bar $1,200 at 2030. Grey arrow rises from lower left to the $1,200 bar, with a white circle outlined in blue holding '+5.3% CAGR'. Right bar chart 'Revenue ($MS)': blue bars $699 (2022), $758 (2023), $809 (2024) and a green bar $1,050 (2030). Grey arrow rising to the upper right with a blue-outlined white circle '+5.2% CAGR'. Thin grey baseline under each chart. Vertical dotted grey separators at about x 49% and x 73%. BrightView wordmark logo in blue with a green stylized leaf mark.

### Slide 30

**Text**

National Accounts: A Significant Strategic Growth Opportunity
Tech Enablement
Measure Progress
Single Point of Contact
Key
Accounts
Aligned Partnership

30

**Layout**

Title at top left with a short lime-green rule beneath it. A lightbulb diagram is centred in the slide, with five circular icons arranged in an arc around its upper half and a coloured label beside each circle. A full-width green banner with centred white text sits near the bottom, the BrightView logo is at bottom left and the slide number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Lightbulb diagram: a light grey (#F2F2F2) bulb with a grey circular ring at its centre holding the text 'Key Accounts'. The bulb has a trapezoid neck and a base of two rounded bars with a blue-teal-green-magenta-orange horizontal gradient. A thick segmented ring arc surrounds the top of the bulb in five colours: bottom-left blue, left dark teal, top teal-green, right magenta, bottom-right orange. Five solid circles connect to the ring by short stubs. Top circle, teal-green (#00AD97), white line icon of a gear with circuit nodes, labelled Tech Enablement. Upper-right circle, magenta (#C800A1), white line icon of a person climbing steps and holding a flag, labelled Single Point of Contact. Lower-right circle, orange (#FF9E1B), white line icon of a handshake over connected nodes, labelled Targeted Accounts. Upper-left circle, dark teal (#007177), white line icon of a location pin with chevrons, labelled Measure Progress. Lower-left circle, blue (#0072CE), white line icon of a target with an arrow, labelled Aligned Partnership. A green gradient banner spans the width above the footer. The BrightView logo has a blue wordmark and a lime-green stylised tree mark.

**Design**

Background: solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold blue #0072CE Arial. Labels in bold Arial, each matching its circle colour. Centre text bold dark grey #313335. Banner is a vertical gradient from light lime #8DC63F to darker green #5CA032 with bold white Arial text. Accent rule lime #8BC400. Flat vector style, no shadows except a faint grey ring around the bulb centre. Title about 5% of slide height, labels about 3.3%.

**Layout**

Title top-left. Centre circular cycle diagram with a ring of four arrows around a central blue dollar/pie badge. Four icon-and-label groups sit in the corners: two on the left (right-aligned text), two on the right (left-aligned text), each joined to the ring by a dotted line. Logo bottom-left, page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Central circular ring made of four light-grey gradient arc arrows (about #F0F0F0 to #D6D6D6) with white gaps between them, each with a light-blue triangular arrowhead (about #5BB8E8) running clockwise: top-left arrow pointing right at top, right arc pointing down, bottom arc pointing left, left arc pointing up. Inside the ring is a blue gradient circle (#7DCBEA to #2FA9DE) with white outline rings and a doughnut-chart pictogram with segments and a white $ in the centre. Curved blue text 'STRATEGIC CAPITAL ALLOCATION' follows the lower inside of the ring. Four light-blue (#6EC6EC) line icons: top-left a plant growing from a pot with a dollar coin as the flower; top-right a brick wall with a trowel; bottom-left a handshake; bottom-right a map pin with ripples. Each icon has a light-blue horizontal bar beneath it (above it for the bottom icons' labels). Grey dotted horizontal lines with grey dot ends connect the labels to the ring at about y 33% and y 73%. BrightView logo bottom-left: blue wordmark with a green stylised tree/B mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white background. Title in bold blue #0072CE Arial, about 5.2% of slide height. Labels in regular Arial grey #63666A, about 3.3% of height, with the parenthetical smaller at about 3%. Accents are light blue #6EC6EC and #5BB8E8; page number in bold green #8BC400. Gradients in the ring arrows and central badge; no shadows or rounded boxes.

**Layout**

White slide with title at top left, a short green rule beneath, and a two-line grey subtitle. Below are two side-by-side chart panels separated by a vertical dotted grey line at the centre; each has a coloured heading on the left. BrightView logo sits bottom left and the page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Left: blue column chart 'Adjusted Free Cash Flow' with six columns (2018 $106, 2019 $87, 2021 $97, 2022 $7, 2023 $80, 2024 $145), white value labels inside the columns, a thin grey baseline, no gridlines, no visible y-axis, category labels under the columns and a blue square legend key 'Adjusted Free Cash Flow'. Right: teal combo chart with six teal columns of Net Debt in $ millions (2018 $1,149, 2019 $1,131, 2021 $1,056, 2022 $1,375, 2023 $871, 2024 $737) and an orange line for Leverage Ratio. The ratio values are 3.8x, 3.7x, 3.5x, 4.8x, 2.9x and 2.3x, printed in bold grey above the columns. The secondary axis runs 0.0 to 5.5 and the primary axis 0 to 1,500, with both sets of axis labels hidden in white text. The legend below shows an orange line 'Leverage Ratio' and a teal square 'Net Debt'. A short lime-green horizontal bar sits under the title. The BrightView logo has blue wordmark text and a lime-green stylised 'b' plant mark. A vertical dotted grey divider separates the panels.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Plain white background. Title in bold Arial-style sans-serif, blue #0072CE, about 5.2% of slide height. Subtitle in bold grey #63666A at about 3.7%. Left chart heading in blue #0072CE and right heading in teal #00AD97, both bold at about 3.3%. Bars are flat with no shadows or gradients. Blue bars #0072CE, teal bars #00AD97, orange line #FF9E1B at about 3px. Axis and legend labels in regular grey at about 2.6%. Accent green #8BC400 for the rule and page number.

### Slide 33

**Review notes**

- Worth studying: The revenue path from FY24 to FY30 is built as a stacked bridge with each driver labeled by its contribution and growth rate, separating organic and acquisition growth clearly.
- Worth keeping: Keep the action titles with a green takeaway bar, the single-message chart slides 22 and 23, and the labeled waterfall on slide 33 with CAGR brackets.

**Text**

Path to ~$4B in Revenue
1
Land Maintenance
~3% to ~6% Growth per Annum (Retention, Route Density, Cross Selling, Ancillary Offerings)
2
Development
~4% to ~5% Growth per Annum (Continued momentum in backlog)
3
Accretive M&A
~$400M to ~$500M in Accretive Mergers & Acquisitions
6.3% CAGR
4.2% CAGR
~$4,000
~$450

BrightView
33

**Layout**

Title top-left. Three full-width rows stacked below the title, each with a blue gradient label bar on the left and a light grey description bar on the right. Below, a light grey icon box on the left and a waterfall chart across the right. Footer with BrightView logo bottom-left, footnotes beside it, green page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Waterfall bar chart with six categories and no value axis, with a thin grey baseline. Bars: FY24 grey $2,770; Land blue floating ~$540; Development blue floating ~$230; FY30 blue total $3,550; M&A teal floating ~$450; FY30 w/ M&A teal total ~$4,000. Thin dashed grey connector lines link bar tops. Grey bracket arrows with dark rounded labels: 6.3% CAGR runs from FY24 up and across to the final bar, and 4.2% CAGR runs from FY24 across to the FY30 bar, each ending in a downward arrowhead. Numbered white circles with blue outline (1, 2, 3) at the left ends of the blue bars. Blue line icon of rising bar chart with an upward arrow and a dollar-sign circle in the grey box. BrightView logo with blue wordmark and green leaf mark.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bold blue #0072CE Arial. Label bars have a blue gradient from #0072CE to #004C97 with white bold text. Grey description text #63666A, with the parenthetical in italics. Chart bar colors are grey #808080, blue #0072CE and teal #007177. CAGR labels are dark #363636 with white text and rounded corners. Page number is green #8BC400. Flat style with no shadows.

**Layout**

Title at top left. Three horizontal banner rows stacked beneath it, each with a blue gradient label on the left with a numbered circle and a light grey description bar to the right. Below, a grey box with icon and label sits at left, and a waterfall chart fills the lower right with CAGR callouts above it. Logo and footnote at bottom left, slide number at bottom right.

**Visuals**

Three white-filled circles with blue outline and blue numerals 1, 2, 3 at the left end of the banner rows. A waterfall bar chart on white with a thin grey baseline. Bars: FY24 grey $325 with 11.7% inside; Top-Line Growth blue floating bar ~$160 labelled +~150bps; Efficiencies & SG&A Leverage blue ~$150 bar with a lighter periwinkle negative ~($50) bar below it, labelled ~$100 above and +~250bps below, with small blue right-pointing triangles pointing to the labels 'FSM, Fleet, Procurement' and 'Seller Investments'; FY30 blue bar $585 with ~16% inside; M&A teal floating bar ~$65 labelled +30~50bps; FY30 w/ M&A teal bar ~$650 with ~16%+ inside. Thin dashed grey connector lines link bar tops. Two grey bracket arrows with dark rounded-rectangle labels: 10.3% CAGR runs from the FY24 bar up and across to an arrow pointing down at the FY30 bar; 12.3% CAGR runs from FY24 across the top to an arrow pointing down at the FY30 w/ M&A bar. Left grey box contains a blue outline icon of ascending bar columns with an upward arrow and a gear. BrightView logo with blue wordmark and green stylised B mark at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. Clean white corporate slide. Title in bold blue #0072CE Arial. Banner labels use blue gradient (#0072CE to #00509A) with white bold text; description bars light grey #F2F2F2 with grey #63666A regular text. Teal #007177 for M&A bars and bps labels, grey #808080 for the FY24 bar, dark charcoal #363636 for CAGR labels with white text and rounded corners. Grey #808080 bracket lines about 3px. Sans-serif Arial throughout. Title about 5% of slide height, body about 2.8%.

**Layout**

Title at top left with a short green rule beneath, then a grey subtitle line. Below is a 3-column by 2-row matrix: left icon/label column, then three light grey panels (Current, Future, Opportunity) with header bars, separated by a dotted horizontal line and grey chevron arrows between columns. BrightView logo bottom left, page number bottom right.

**Visuals**

Two blue circular outline icons at left: a routing/flow icon (triangles connected by lines with an arrow) and a satellite dish with signal waves. Current column: top a faded photo of a whiteboard with handwritten schedules on a blue wall; bottom a screenshot of a dense spreadsheet schedule with green header row. Future column: top a Gantt scheduling software screenshot (Tue, November 12, 2024) with green, orange, pink and blue job blocks across three teams; bottom a smartphone screenshot of FieldServiceMobile app on a dark screen with blue phone casing listing appointments. Pale grey chevron arrows point right between the columns (two rows, two gaps). Column headers are gradient bars: teal for Current and Future, green for Opportunity. Dotted grey horizontal line separates the two rows. BrightView logo with green bracket-like mark at bottom left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title bright blue #0072CE bold Arial about 5.2% of slide height. Subtitle grey #63666A bold Arial about 3.7%. Short green rule #8BC400 under title. Panels light grey #F2F2F2. Header bars with vertical gradient teal (#00C9B0 to #007177) and green (#8BC400 to #6E9F00), white bold Arial 3%. Row labels blue #0072CE bold Arial 2.6%. Future text teal #007177 bold 2.6%. Opportunity text lime green #8BC400 bold 3.7%, centered. Flat style, no shadows.

38

**Layout**

Title top-left. A large donut chart fills the left and centre, with category labels and icons placed around its edge. A light grey rectangle with a chevron on its left edge sits on the right and holds centred text. The footer has the BrightView logo at bottom-left, a footnote beside it and the slide number at bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Donut chart with four segments running clockwise from 12 o'clock: Frontline Labor about 40% (blue #0072CE), Materials & Commodities about 30% (green #8BC400), SG&A about 20% (orange #FF9E1B), Fleet about 10% (teal #007177). Each segment has a white bold percentage label. The hole is white. Line icons sit beside the labels: a teal delivery truck with speed lines above Fleet; an orange person with a dollar speech bubble above SG&A; a blue pair of people with exchange arrows above Frontline Labor; a green stack of boxes on a pallet beside Materials & Commodities. A grey gradient chevron arrow points right at the left edge of the grey box. The BrightView wordmark in blue with a green tree mark is at bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. The title is bright blue #0072CE in bold Arial/Helvetica-style sans-serif, about 5.2% of slide height. The labels are bold sans-serif in the segment colours at about 3.3%. The callout text is grey #63666A regular Arial at about 4.4%. The big figure is blue bold at 7.4%. The grey box is #F2F2F2 with no border or shadow. The footnote is small grey text and the page number is bold green. Flat style with no shadows.

39

**Layout**

Title top-left with a short green rule beneath, then a two-line grey subtitle spanning the width. Below, three columns: left donut under a red banner, middle donut under a green banner (separated by a dotted vertical line), and a grey case-study panel on the right. Legend row at bottom centre-left, BrightView logo bottom-left, page number bottom-right.

**Visuals**

Left: red pentagon-style banner with left-pointing notch and a white 'no' (prohibition) circle icon at left; below it a donut chart with about 25% green (Strategic Spend, top-right quarter) and about 75% light grey (Non-Strategic Spend). Middle: green banner with right-pointing arrow tip; below it a full green donut (100% Strategic Spend). A vertical dotted grey line separates the two donuts. Legend: green square 'Strategic Spend', grey square 'Non-Strategic Spend'. Right: light grey panel with a horizontal bar chart on a thin vertical grey axis: Branch A (Non-Preferred) green bar labelled $22; Branch B (Non-Preferred) green bar labelled $20, slightly shorter; Preferred Vendor (Fixed Price + Rebate) stacked bar with light green $17 (white label) plus dark green $2 segment (label $2 in dark green), total $19. A dotted dark-grey rounded-corner-less rectangle encloses the Preferred Vendor row and the closing statement. BrightView logo with blue wordmark and green stylised B glyph bottom-left.

**Design**

Background: Solid white #FFFFFF. White background. Title in bright blue #0072CE bold Arial sans-serif, about 5% of slide height. Subtitle and panel text in grey #63666A bold Arial about 3.7%. Accent green #8BC400 for rule, bars, donut and page number; red #C00000 for the legacy label and banner gradient (red #D40000 to dark red #8E0000); green banner gradient from #9CE000 to #6B9A00. Panel fill #F2F2F2. Dotted outline in grey. Flat style, no shadows.

**Visuals**

Combo chart: four blue columns (1st to 4th Quartile) showing Average Maintenance Branch Revenue labelled $5, $8, $11, $17 (in millions), rising left to right, with white labels inside the bars. An orange line with circular markers (EBITDA Margin %) rises from 11% to 12% to 14% to 16%, labels above the points. A green line with circular markers (SG&A %) falls from about the highest point at the 1st quartile (about 17%) to about 15% at the 2nd, crossing the orange line at the 3rd quartile (about 14%), and ends about 13% at the 4th; no values printed.
